[54] user: 样板森林边缘：按 Sample 左侧森林的单株树密排方式。
样板混合树组：按 Sample 中散树/小树林混排方式。
红顶蓝墙房屋：按 Sample 左上房屋组合。
灰顶木墙房屋：按 Sample 中部/右侧小屋组合。
城堡门楼：按 Sample 右下城堡的塔楼 + 中央门楼复合结构。
泥地区块：按 Sample 右上泥地块组合。和原图不同，需要每个板块和原图一致

  display: flex;
  gap: 6px;
  overflow-x: auto;
  padding: 10px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: #131a20;
}

.category-tabs button {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  padding: 0 10px;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.asset-list {
  min-height: 0;
  overflow: auto;
  padding: 10px 12px;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(46px, 1fr));
  align-content: start;
  justify-content: start;
  gap: 8px;
}

.asset-card {
  width: 100%;
  aspect-ratio: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  min-height: 0;
  padding: 4px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 7px;
  background: #202a32;
  color: var(--ink);
  text-align: center;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.asset-card.active {
  border-color: var(--accent);
  background: #2c352f;
}

.asset-card img {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  max-width: 42px;
  max-height: 42px;
  object-fit: contain;
  object-position: center;
  image-rendering: pixelated;
  display: block;
  border-radius: 4px;
  background: #2a343b;
}

.asset-card span {
  position: absolute;
  width: 1px;
  height: 1px;
  overflow: hidden;
  clip: rect(0, 0, 0, 0);
}

.asset-card small {
  display: none;
}

.asset-card.asset-note {
  aspect-ratio: auto;
  width: 118px;
  min-height: 68px;
  padding: 10px;
  place-items: start;
  text-align: left;
}

  }
}

func _add_player(tile_position: Vector2i) -> void:
	player = CharacterBody2D.new()
	player.name = "Player_CharacterBody2D"
	var clamped_tile := Vector2i(
		clamp(tile_position.x, 1, max(1, active_map_w - 2)),
		clamp(tile_position.y, 1, max(1, active_map_h - 2))
	)
	player.position = Vector2(clamped_tile.x * active_cell_px + active_cell_px * 0.5, clamped_tile.y * active_cell_px + active_cell_px * 0.5)
	player.collision_layer = 1
	player.collision_mask = 1
	player.z_index = 20
	map_root.add_child(player)

	var collision := CollisionShape2D.new()
	var circle := CircleShape2D.new()
	circle.radius = 6
	collision.shape = circle
	player.add_child(collision)

	player_sprite = AnimatedSprite2D.new()
	player_sprite.name = "AnimatedPunyWarrior"
	player_sprite.sprite_frames = _create_character_frames()
	player_sprite.animation = "idle_down"
	player_sprite.scale = Vector2(0.85, 0.85)
	player_sprite.texture_filter = CanvasItem.TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST
	player.add_child(player_sprite)

	camera = Camera2D.new()
	camera.name = "FollowCamera"
	camera.zoom = Vector2.ONE if current_map.id == MAP_EDITOR_JSON else Vector2(1.6, 1.6)
	camera.position_smoothing_enabled = true
	camera.position_smoothing_speed = 7.5
	camera.limit_left = 0
	camera.limit_top = 0
	camera.limit_right = active_map_w * active_cell_px
	camera.limit_bottom = active_map_h * active_cell_px
	player.add_child(camera)
	camera.make_current()

func _puny_frame(coord: Vector2i) -> AtlasTexture:
	return _atlas_custom(CHARACTER_SHEET, Rect2(coord.x * 32, coord.y * 32, 32, 32))

func _update_character_animation(direction: Vector2) -> void:
	if player_sprite == null:
		return
	if direction == Vector2.ZERO:
		player_sprite.play("idle_%s" % player_facing)
		return
	if abs(direction.x) > abs(direction.y):
		player_facing = "right" if direction.x > 0 else "left"
	else:
		player_facing = "down" if direction.y > 0 else "up"
	player_sprite.play("walk_%s" % player_facing)

[54] user: 样板森林边缘：按 Sample 左侧森林的单株树密排方式。
样板混合树组：按 Sample 中散树/小树林混排方式。
红顶蓝墙房屋：按 Sample 左上房屋组合。
灰顶木墙房屋：按 Sample 中部/右侧小屋组合。
城堡门楼：按 Sample 右下城堡的塔楼 + 中央门楼复合结构。
泥地区块：按 Sample 右上泥地块组合。和原图不同，需要每个板块和原图一致

func _add_player(tile_position: Vector2i) -> void:
	player = CharacterBody2D.new()
	player.name = "Player_CharacterBody2D"
	var clamped_tile := Vector2i(
		clamp(tile_position.x, 1, max(1, active_map_w - 2)),
		clamp(tile_position.y, 1, max(1, active_map_h - 2))
	)
	player.position = Vector2(clamped_tile.x * active_cell_px + active_cell_px * 0.5, clamped_tile.y * active_cell_px + active_cell_px * 0.5)
	player.collision_layer = 1
	player.collision_mask = 1
	player.z_index = 20
	map_root.add_child(player)

	var collision := CollisionShape2D.new()
	var circle := CircleShape2D.new()
	circle.radius = 6
	collision.shape = circle
	player.add_child(collision)

	player_sprite = AnimatedSprite2D.new()
	player_sprite.name = "AnimatedPunyWarrior"
	player_sprite.sprite_frames = _create_character_frames()
	player_sprite.animation = "idle_down"
	player_sprite.scale = Vector2(0.85, 0.85)
	player_sprite.texture_filter = CanvasItem.TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST
	player.add_child(player_sprite)

	camera = Camera2D.new()
	camera.name = "FollowCamera"
	camera.zoom = Vector2.ONE if current_map.id == MAP_EDITOR_JSON else Vector2(1.6, 1.6)
	camera.position_smoothing_enabled = true
	camera.position_smoothing_speed = 7.5
	camera.limit_left = 0
	camera.limit_top = 0
	camera.limit_right = active_map_w * active_cell_px
	camera.limit_bottom = active_map_h * active_cell_px
	player.add_child(camera)
	camera.make_current()

func _create_character_frames() -> SpriteFrames:

func _puny_frame(coord: Vector2i) -> AtlasTexture:
	return _atlas_custom(CHARACTER_SHEET, Rect2(coord.x * 32, coord.y * 32, 32, 32))

func _update_character_animation(direction: Vector2) -> void:
	if player_sprite == null:
		return
	if direction == Vector2.ZERO:
		player_sprite.play("idle_%s" % player_facing)
		return
	if abs(direction.x) > abs(direction.y):
		player_facing = "right" if direction.x > 0 else "left"
	else:
		player_facing = "down" if direction.y > 0 else "up"
	player_sprite.play("walk_%s" % player_facing)

func _add_game_ui() -> void:
	ui_layer = CanvasLayer.new()
	ui_layer.name = "MapRuntimeUI"
	add_child(ui_layer)

  padding: 10px;

}

func _add_player(tile_position: Vector2i) -> void:
	player = CharacterBody2D.new()
	player.name = "Player_CharacterBody2D"
	var clamped_tile := Vector2i(
		clamp(tile_position.x, 1, max(1, active_map_w - 2)),
		clamp(tile_position.y, 1, max(1, active_map_h - 2))
	)
	player.position = Vector2(clamped_tile.x * active_cell_px + active_cell_px * 0.5, clamped_tile.y * active_cell_px + active_cell_px * 0.5)
	player.collision_layer = 1
	player.collision_mask = 1
	player.z_index = 20
	map_root.add_child(player)

	var collision := CollisionShape2D.new()
	var circle := CircleShape2D.new()
	circle.radius = 6
	collision.shape = circle
	player.add_child(collision)

	player_sprite = AnimatedSprite2D.new()
	player_sprite.name = "AnimatedPunyWarrior"
	player_sprite.sprite_frames = _create_character_frames()
	player_sprite.animation = "idle_down"
	player_sprite.scale = Vector2(0.85, 0.85)
	player_sprite.texture_filter = CanvasItem.TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST
	player.add_child(player_sprite)

	camera = Camera2D.new()
	camera.name = "FollowCamera"
	camera.zoom = Vector2.ONE if current_map.id == MAP_EDITOR_JSON else Vector2(1.6, 1.6)
	camera.position_smoothing_enabled = true
	camera.position_smoothing_speed = 7.5
	camera.limit_left = 0
	camera.limit_top = 0
	camera.limit_right = active_map_w * active_cell_px
	camera.limit_bottom = active_map_h * active_cell_px
	player.add_child(camera)
	camera.make_current()

func _create_character_frames() -> SpriteFrames:
	var frames := SpriteFrames.new()
	for anim in ["idle_down", "idle_up", "idle_right", "idle_left", "walk_down", "walk_up", "walk_right", "walk_left"]:
		frames.add_animation(anim)
		frames.set_animation_speed(anim, 8.0)
		frames.set_animation_loop(anim, true)
	frames.add_frame("idle_down", _puny_frame(Vector2i(0, 0)))
	frames.add_frame("idle_left", _puny_frame(Vector2i(0, 2)))
	frames.add_frame("idle_right", _puny_frame(Vector2i(0, 3)))
	frames.add_frame("idle_up", _puny_frame(Vector2i(0, 4)))
	for col in range(0, 6):

func _puny_frame(coord: Vector2i) -> AtlasTexture:
	return _atlas_custom(CHARACTER_SHEET, Rect2(coord.x * 32, coord.y * 32, 32, 32))

func _update_character_animation(direction: Vector2) -> void:
	if player_sprite == null:
		return
	if direction == Vector2.ZERO:
		player_sprite.play("idle_%s" % player_facing)
		return
	if abs(direction.x) > abs(direction.y):
		player_facing = "right" if direction.x > 0 else "left"
	else:
		player_facing = "down" if direction.y > 0 else "up"
	player_sprite.play("walk_%s" % player_facing)

func _add_game_ui() -> void:

	ui_layer.name = "MapRuntimeUI"

[54] user: 样板森林边缘：按 Sample 左侧森林的单株树密排方式。
样板混合树组：按 Sample 中散树/小树林混排方式。
红顶蓝墙房屋：按 Sample 左上房屋组合。
灰顶木墙房屋：按 Sample 中部/右侧小屋组合。
城堡门楼：按 Sample 右下城堡的塔楼 + 中央门楼复合结构。
泥地区块：按 Sample 右上泥地块组合。和原图不同，需要每个板块和原图一致

  display: flex;
  gap: 6px;
  overflow-x: auto;
  padding: 10px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: #131a20;
}

.category-tabs button {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  padding: 0 10px;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.asset-list {
  min-height: 0;
  overflow: auto;
  padding: 10px 12px;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(46px, 1fr));
  align-content: start;
  justify-content: start;
  gap: 8px;
}

.asset-card {
  width: 100%;
  aspect-ratio: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  min-height: 0;
  padding: 4px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 7px;
  background: #202a32;
  color: var(--ink);
  text-align: center;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.asset-card.active {
  border-color: var(--accent);
  background: #2c352f;
}

.asset-card img {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  max-width: 42px;
  max-height: 42px;
  object-fit: contain;
  object-position: center;
  image-rendering: pixelated;
  display: block;
  border-radius: 4px;
  background: #2a343b;
}

.asset-card span {
  position: absolute;
  width: 1px;
  height: 1px;
  overflow: hidden;
  clip: rect(0, 0, 0, 0);
}

.asset-card small {
  display: none;
}

.asset-card.asset-note {
  aspect-ratio: auto;
  width: 118px;
  min-height: 68px;
  padding: 10px;
  place-items: start;
  text-align: left;
}

func _add_player(tile_position: Vector2i) -> void:
	player = CharacterBody2D.new()
	player.name = "Player_CharacterBody2D"
	var clamped_tile := Vector2i(
		clamp(tile_position.x, 1, max(1, active_map_w - 2)),
		clamp(tile_position.y, 1, max(1, active_map_h - 2))
	)
	player.position = Vector2(clamped_tile.x * active_cell_px + active_cell_px * 0.5, clamped_tile.y * active_cell_px + active_cell_px * 0.5)
	player.collision_layer = 1
	player.collision_mask = 1
	player.z_index = 20
	map_root.add_child(player)

	var collision := CollisionShape2D.new()
	var circle := CircleShape2D.new()
	circle.radius = 6
	collision.shape = circle
	player.add_child(collision)

	player_sprite = AnimatedSprite2D.new()
	player_sprite.name = "AnimatedPunyWarrior"
	player_sprite.sprite_frames = _create_character_frames()
	player_sprite.animation = "idle_down"
	player_sprite.scale = Vector2(0.85, 0.85)
	player_sprite.texture_filter = CanvasItem.TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST
	player.add_child(player_sprite)

	camera = Camera2D.new()
	camera.name = "FollowCamera"
	camera.zoom = Vector2.ONE if current_map.id == MAP_EDITOR_JSON else Vector2(1.6, 1.6)
	camera.position_smoothing_enabled = true
	camera.position_smoothing_speed = 7.5
	camera.limit_left = 0
	camera.limit_top = 0
	camera.limit_right = active_map_w * active_cell_px
	camera.limit_bottom = active_map_h * active_cell_px
	player.add_child(camera)
	camera.make_current()

func _create_character_frames() -> SpriteFrames:
	var frames := SpriteFrames.new()
	for anim in ["idle_down", "idle_up", "idle_right", "idle_left", "walk_down", "walk_up", "walk_right", "walk_left"]:
		frames.add_animation(anim)

		frames.set_animation_loop(anim, true)

	frames.add_frame("idle_right", _puny_frame(Vector2i(0, 3)))
	frames.add_frame("idle_up", _puny_frame(Vector2i(0, 4)))

func _puny_frame(coord: Vector2i) -> AtlasTexture:
	return _atlas_custom(CHARACTER_SHEET, Rect2(coord.x * 32, coord.y * 32, 32, 32))

func _update_character_animation(direction: Vector2) -> void:
	if player_sprite == null:
		return
	if direction == Vector2.ZERO:
		player_sprite.play("idle_%s" % player_facing)
		return
	if abs(direction.x) > abs(direction.y):
		player_facing = "right" if direction.x > 0 else "left"
	else:
		player_facing = "down" if direction.y > 0 else "up"
	player_sprite.play("walk_%s" % player_facing)

func _add_game_ui() -> void:

[54] user: 样板森林边缘：按 Sample 左侧森林的单株树密排方式。
样板混合树组：按 Sample 中散树/小树林混排方式。
红顶蓝墙房屋：按 Sample 左上房屋组合。
灰顶木墙房屋：按 Sample 中部/右侧小屋组合。
城堡门楼：按 Sample 右下城堡的塔楼 + 中央门楼复合结构。
泥地区块：按 Sample 右上泥地块组合。和原图不同，需要每个板块和原图一致

<html lang="zh-CN">
<head>

  <title>GameGmeta Map Editor</title>

</head>
<body>
  <main class="editor-shell" aria-label="GameGmeta grid map editor">
    <header class="topbar">
      <div>
        <h1>GameGmeta 地图编辑器</h1>
        <p>从右侧 tiny 与收藏素材库拖拽到网格，素材会叠加在草地底层之上。</p>
      </div>
      <div class="toolbar" aria-label="Editor actions">
        <button type="button" id="loadDemo">加载 Demo</button>
        <button type="button" id="fillGrass">铺满草地</button>
        <button type="button" id="clearMap">清空地图</button>
        <button type="button" id="exportMap">导出 JSON</button>
        <button type="button" id="exportGodotMap">导出 Godot JSON</button>
        <button type="button" id="playGodotMap" class="primary-action">进入游戏</button>
      </div>
    </header>

    <section class="workspace">
      <section class="canvas-panel" aria-label="Map canvas">
        <div class="canvas-head">
          <div>

            <span id="selectionLabel">当前选择：草地</span>

          <div class="canvas-stats">
            <span>32 x 20</span>
            <span>32px 网格</span>

        <div class="map-frame">
          <div id="mapBoard" class="map-board" role="application" aria-label="Editable tile grid"></div>

      <aside class="asset-panel" aria-label="Asset library">
        <div class="asset-head">
          <div>

        <div class="category-tabs" id="categoryTabs" aria-label="Asset categories"></div>
        <div class="asset-list" id="assetList"></div>
      </aside>
    </section>

    <section class="statusbar" aria-live="polite">
      <span id="statusText">拖拽素材到地图，或先点选素材再点击格子放置。</span>

:root {
  color-scheme: dark;
  --bg: #10151a;
  --panel: #171e24;
  --panel-2: #202932;
  --ink: #f2f6ee;
  --muted: #9aa8a0;
  --line: #33414a;
  --line-strong: #52636d;
  --accent: #e4b35d;
  --accent-2: #7ed481;
  --danger: #d86c63;
  --cell: 32px;
  --board-cols: 32;
  --board-rows: 20;
  --asset-card: 48px;
  --asset-thumb: 38px;
}

* {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

html,
body {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  margin: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: var(--bg);
  color: var(--ink);
  font-family: Inter, ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
}

button,
input,
select {
  font: inherit;
}

button {
  min-height: 34px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line-strong);
  border-radius: 6px;
  background: #25313a;
  color: var(--ink);
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 760;
  cursor: pointer;
}

button:hover,
button.active {
  border-color: var(--accent);
  color: #fff0c9;
}

button.primary-action {
  border-color: #5f9d61;
  background: #2d6f38;
  color: #f1fff0;
}

button.primary-action:hover {
  border-color: var(--accent-2);
  color: #ffffff;
}

.editor-shell {
  width: 100vw;
  height: 100vh;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-rows: auto 1fr auto;
  background:
    linear-gradient(rgba(255, 255, 255, .025) 1px, transparent 1px),
    linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, .025) 1px, transparent 1px),
    #10151a;
  background-size: 32px 32px;
}

.topbar {
  height: 84px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 24px;
  padding: 14px 18px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: rgba(18, 24, 29, .96);
}

h1 {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 22px;
  line-height: 1.1;
  letter-spacing: 0;
}

.topbar p {
  margin: 8px 0 0;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 13px;
}

.toolbar {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  just<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="181" />{
  display: block;
  margin-top: 5px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 12px;
}

.canvas-stats {
  display: flex;
  gap: 8px;
}

.canvas-stats span {
  margin: 0;
  padding: 6px 9px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 999px;
  color: #c9d5cc;
  background: #11181d;
}

.map-frame {
  min-height: 0;
  overflow: auto;
  padding: 18px;
  background: #0c1115;
}

.map-board {
  position: relative;
  width: calc(var(--board-cols) * var(--cell));
  height: calc(var(--board-rows) * var(--cell));
  margin: 0 auto;
  border: 2px solid #5e6f78;
  background-color: #79bf63;
  background-image:
    linear-gradient(rgba(0, 0, 0, .18) 1px, transparent 1px),
    linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(0, 0, 0, .18) 1px, transparent 1px);
  background-size: var(--cell) var(--cell);
  image-rendering: pixelated;
  overflow: hidden;
  box-shadow: 0 24px 70px rgba(0, 0, 0, .36);
}

.grid-cell {
  position: absolute;
  width: var(--cell);
  height: var(--cell);
  background-repeat: repeat;
  background-size: var(--cell) var(--cell);
  image-rendering: pixelated;
}

.grid-cell.drop-target {
  outline: 2px solid rgba(255, 236, 157, .92);
  outline-offset: -2px;
}

.stamp {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 3;
  display: block;
  image-rendering: pixelated;
  object-fit: contain;
  user-select: none;
  cursor: move;
}

.stamp.selected {
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 0 #fff) drop-shadow(0 0 8px rgba(255, 220, 115, .95));
}

.stamp.blocking::after {
  content: "";
}

.asset-panel {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-rows: auto auto 1fr;
}

.asset-head {
  align-items: start;
}

.asset-controls {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(180px, 1fr) minmax(120px, 160px);
  gap: 8px;
}

#assetSearch,
#packageSelect {
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 34px;
  padding: 0 10px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line-strong);
  border-radius: 6px;
  background: #10171c;
  color: var(--ink);
  outline: none;
}

#assetSearch:focus,
#packageSelect:focus {
  border-color: var(--accent);
}

board.addEventListener("drop", (event) => {
  event.preventDefault();
  const rect = board.getBoundingClientRect();
  const x = Math.floor((event.clientX - rect.left) / CELL);
  const y = Math.floor((event.clientY - rect.top) / CELL);
  if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= COLS || y >= ROWS) return;

  const stampId = Number(event.dataTransfer.getData("application/x-stamp-id"));
  if (stampId) {
    const stamp = stamps.find((item) => item.id === stampId);
    if (!stamp) return;
    const asset = assetById(stamp.assetId);
    if (!asset) return;
    const origin = clampOrigin(asset, x, y);
    const removed = removeOverlappingStamps({
      x: origin.x,
      y: origin.y,
      w: Math.max(1, stamp.w),
      h: Math.max(1, stamp.h),
    }, stamp.id);
    stamp.x = origin.x;
    stamp.y = origin.y;
    selectedStampId = stamp.id;
    renderStamps();
    statusText.textContent = removed > 0
      ? `已移动 ${asset.name}，并替换 ${removed} 个上层素材：${origin.x}, ${origin.y}`
      : `已移动 ${asset.name}：${origin.x}, ${origin.y}`;
    return;
  }

  const asset = assetById(event.dataTransfer.getData("text/plain"));
  if (asset) placeAsset(asset, x, y);
});

function fillDefaultTerrain() {
  const asset = defaultTile();
  for (let y = 0; y < ROWS; y += 1) {
    for (let x = 0; x < COLS; x += 1) {
      terrain[y][x] = asset?.id || null;
    }
  }
  renderTerrain();
  statusText.textContent = `已将地图版面铺满 ${asset?.name || "默认地块"}。`;
}

function clearMap() {
  fillDefaultTerrain();
  stamps.length = 0;
  selectedStampId = null;
  renderStamps();
  statusText.textContent = "已清除之前的地图内容。";
}

function normalizeTerrainCell(cell) {
  if (typeof cell === "string") return cell;
  if (cell && typeof cell === "object") return cell.assetId || null;
  return null;
}

function applyMapData(data, label = <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1432" />ener("click", exportMap);
  document.getElementById("exportGodotMap").addEventListener("click", exportGodotMap);
  document.getElementById("playGodotMap").addEventListener("click", playGodotMap);
  assetSearch.addEventListener("input", renderAssetList);
  packageSelect.addEventListener("change", () => {
    activePackage = packageSelect.value;
    activeCategory = "全部";
    buildCategories();
    renderAssetList();
  });

  window.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
    if (event.key === "Delete" && selectedStampId != null) {
      const index = stamps.findIndex((stamp) => stamp.id === selectedStampId);
      if (index >= 0) {
        const asset = assetById(stamps[index].assetId);
        stamps.splice(index, 1);
        selectedStampId = null;
        renderStamps();
        statusText.textContent = `已删除 ${asset.name}。`;
      }
    }
    if (event.key === "Escape") {
      selectedStampId = null;
      renderStamps();
      statusText.textContent = "已取消选择。";
    }
  });
}

async function loadLibrary() {
  const response = await fetch(MANIFEST_URL);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`无法读取 Kenney manifest: ${response.status}`);
  const manifest = await response.json();
  ASSETS = tinyOnlyAssets(manifest.assets || []);
  PACKAGES = tinyOnlyPackages(manifest.packages || [], ASSETS);
  if (!PACKAGES.some((pack) => pack.name === activePackage)) activePackage = PACKAGES[0]?.name || "全部";
  assetLibraryMeta.textContent = `Tiny、AI tiny 与收藏素材 ${ASSETS.length} 个 / ${PACKAGES.length} 个素材包`;
  selectedAsset = defaultTile();
}

async function start() {
  bindEvents();
  buildBoard();
  await loadLibrary();
  buildPackageSelect();
  buildCategories();
  fillDefaultTerrain();
  renderAssetList();
  if (selectedAsset) selectAsset(selectedAsset);
  await loadDemoMap();
}

start().catch((error) => {
  console.error(error);
  statusText.textContent = `素材库加载失败：${error.message}`;
});

# Browser

Use this skill for browser automation tasks such as inspecting pages, navigating, testing local apps, clicking, typing, taking screenshots, and reading visible page state. After setup, select the `iab` browser.

Keep browser work in the background by default.

Show the browser when the user's request is primarily to put a page in front of them or let them watch the interaction, such as "open localhost:3000", "go to the docs page", "take me to the PR", "show me the current tab", or "keep the browser open while you test checkout".

Do not show the browser when navigation is only a means to answer a question or verify behavior, such as "check localhost:3000 and tell me whether login works", "inspect the docs page and summarize what changed", or "verify the modal still opens correctly". Localhost targets and ordinary page navigation do not by themselves require visibility.

When the browser should be visible to the user, actually present it with `await (await browser.capabilities.get("visibility")).set(true)`.

If this plugin is listed as available in the session, treat that as mandatory reading before browser work. Open and follow this skill before saying that Browser is unavailable and before falling back to standalone Playwright or Computer Use.

Do not skip this skill just because Computer Use MCP tool calls are directly visible or appear easier to invoke. The presence of Computer Use tools is not evidence that Computer Use is the preferred browser surface.

Before using this skill for the first time in the current conversation context, read<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2463" />cell that never created the binding.

### General guidance

* Minimize interruptions as much as possible. Only ask clarifying questions if you really need to. If a user has an under-specified prompt, try to fulfill it first before asking for more information.
* Remember, the user is asking questions about what they see on the screen. Base your interactions on what is visible to the user (based on DOM and screenshots) rather than programmatically determining what they are talking about. The "first link" on the page is not necessarily the first `a href` in the DOM.
* Try not to over-complicate things. It is okay to click based on node ID if it is not clear how to determine the UI element in Playwright.
* If a tab is already on a given URL, do not call `goto` with the same URL. This will reload the page and may lose any in-progress information the user has provided. When you intentionally need to reload, call `tab.reload()`.
* If browser-use is interrupted because the extension or user took control, do not quote the raw runtime error. Summarize it naturally for the user, for example: "Browser use was stopped in the extension." Avoid internal terms like turn_id, runtime, retry, or plugin error text unless the user asks for details.
* When testing a user's local app on `localhost`, `127.0.0.1`, `::1`, or another local development URL in a framework that does not support hot reloading or hot reloading is disabled, call `tab.reload()` after code or build changes before verifying the UI. After reloading, take a fresh DOM snapshot or screenshot before continuing.
* Do not brute-force undocumented site search URLs, query parameter variants, search engine query grids, or candidate URL arrays unless the user explicitly asks for exhaustive coverage.
* If a guessed URL, search query, or candidate page fails, try at most one new approach. After that, switch to visible page navigation, the site's own search UI, or give the best current answer with uncertainty.

### Screenshots
* If you take a screenshot that the user should see, include the image inline in your Markdown response using Markdown image syntax so the image renders, rather than as a bare link:
  ```md
  ![screenshot](IMAGE_LINK)
  ```
* IMPORTANT: If the user has asked you to take screenshots, you MUST include them as part of your final markdown response.
* If the user has asked you to test a website as part of development, you should take screenshots at key moments and include them in your final response.

## Playwright

Playwright is a critical part of the JavaScript API available to you.

You only have access to a limited subset of the Playwright API, so only call functions that are explicitly defined.
You do have access to `tab.playwright.evaluate(...)`, but only in a read-only page scope.

[54] user: 样板森林边缘：按 Sample 左侧森林的单株树密排方式。
样板混合树组：按 Sample 中散树/小树林混排方式。
红顶蓝墙房屋：按 Sample 左上房屋组合。
灰顶木墙房屋：按 Sample 中部/右侧小屋组合。
城堡门楼：按 Sample 右下城堡的塔楼 + 中央门楼复合结构。
泥地区块：按 Sample 右上泥地块组合。和原图不同，需要每个板块和原图一致

<!doctype html>
<html lang="zh-CN">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
  <title>GameGmeta Map Editor</title>
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="./styles.css">
</head>
<body>
  <main class="editor-shell" aria-label="GameGmeta grid map editor">
    <header class="topbar">
      <div>
        <h1>GameGmeta 地图编辑器</h1>
        <p>从右侧 tiny 与收藏素材库拖拽到网格，素材会叠加在草地底层之上。</p>
      </div>
      <div class="toolbar" aria-label="Editor actions">
        <button type="button" id="loadDemo">加载 Demo</button>
        <button type="button" id="fillGrass">铺满草地</button>
        <button type="button" id="clearMap">清空地图</button>
        <button type="button" id="exportMap">导出 JSON</button>
        <button type="button" id="exportGodotMap">导出 Godot JSON</button>
        <button type="button" id="playGodotMap" class="primary-action">进入游戏</button>
      </div>
    </header>

    <section class="workspace">
      <section class="canvas-panel" aria-label="Map canvas">
        <div class="canvas-head">
          <div>
            <strong>地图版面</strong>
            <span id="selectionLabel">当前选择：草地</span>
          </div>
          <div class="canvas-stats">
            <span>32 x 20</span>
            <span>32px 网格</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="map-frame">
          <div id="mapBoard" class="map-board" role="application" aria-label="Editable tile grid"></div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <aside class="asset-panel" aria-label="Asset library">
        <div class="asset-head">
          <div>
            <strong>素材库</strong>
            <span id="assetLibraryMeta">来自本地 tiny、AI tiny 与收藏素材库</span>
          </div>
          <div class="asset-controls">
            <select id="packageSelect" aria-label="Kenney package"></select>
            <input id="assetSearch" type="search" placeholder="搜索素材">
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="category-tabs" id="categoryTabs" aria-label="Asset categories"></div>
        <div class="asset-list" id="assetList"></div>
      </aside>
    </section>

    <section class="statusbar" aria-live="polite">
      <span id="statusText">拖拽素材到地图，或先点选素材再点击格子放置。</span>
      <span>Delete 删除选中物件，Esc 取消选择。</span>
    </section>
  </main>

  <script type="module" src="./game.js"></script>
</body>
</html>

  --cell: 32px;
  --board-cols: 32;
  --board-rows: 20;
  --asset-card: 48px;
  --asset-thumb: 38px;
}

* {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

html,
body {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  margin: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: var(--bg);
  color: var(--ink);
  font-family: Inter, ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
}

button,
input,
select {
  font: inherit;
}

button {
  min-height: 34px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line-strong);
  border-radius: 6px;
  background: #25313a;
  color: var(--ink);
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 760;
  cursor: pointer;
}

button:hover,
button.active {
  border-color: var(--accent);
  color: #fff0c9;
}

button.primary-action {
  border-color: #5f9d61;
  background: #2d6f38;
  color: #f1fff0;
}

button.primary-action:hover {
  border-color: var(--accent-2);
  color: #ffffff;
}

.editor-shell {
  width: 100vw;
  height: 100vh;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-rows: auto 1fr auto;
  background:
    linear-gradient(rgba(255, 255, 255, .025) 1px, transparent 1px),
    linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, .025) 1px, transparent 1px),
    #10151a;
  background-size: 32px 32px;
}

.topbar {
  height: 84px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 24px;
  padding: 14px 18px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: rgba(18, 24, 29, .96);
}

h1 {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 22px;
  line-height: 1.1;
  letter-spacing: 0;
}

.topbar p {
  margin: 8px 0 0;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 13px;
}

.toolbar {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  just<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="181" />{
  display: block;
  margin-top: 5px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 12px;
}

.canvas-stats {
  display: flex;
  gap: 8px;
}

.canvas-stats span {
  margin: 0;
  padding: 6px 9px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 999px;
  color: #c9d5cc;
  background: #11181d;
}

.map-frame {
  min-height: 0;
  overflow: auto;
  padding: 18px;
  background: #0c1115;
}

.map-board {
  position: relative;
  width: calc(var(--board-cols) * var(--cell));
  height: calc(var(--board-rows) * var(--cell));
  margin: 0 auto;
  border: 2px solid #5e6f78;
  background-color: #79bf63;
  background-image:
    linear-gradient(rgba(0, 0, 0, .18) 1px, transparent 1px),
    linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(0, 0, 0, .18) 1px, transparent 1px);
  background-size: var(--cell) var(--cell);
  image-rendering: pixelated;
  overflow: hidden;
  box-shadow: 0 24px 70px rgba(0, 0, 0, .36);
}

.grid-cell {
  position: absolute;
  width: var(--cell);
  height: var(--cell);
  background-repeat: repeat;
  background-size: var(--cell) var(--cell);
  image-rendering: pixelated;
}

.grid-cell.drop-target {
  outline: 2px solid rgba(255, 236, 157, .92);
  outline-offset: -2px;
}

.stamp {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 3;
  display: block;
  image-rendering: pixelated;
  object-fit: contain;
  user-select: none;
  cursor: move;
}

.stamp.selected {
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 0 #fff) drop-shadow(0 0 8px rgba(255, 220, 115, .95));
}

.stamp.blocking::after {
  content: "";
}

.asset-panel {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-rows: auto auto 1fr;
}

.asset-head {
  align-items: start;
}

.asset-controls {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(180px, 1fr) minmax(120px, 160px);
  gap: 8px;
}

#assetSearch,
#packageSelect {
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 34px;
  padding: 0 10px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line-strong);
  border-radius: 6px;
  background: #10171c;
  color: var(--ink);
  outline: none;
}

#assetSearch:focus,
#packageSelect:focus {
  border-color: var(--accent);
}

board.addEventListener("drop", (event) => {
  event.preventDefault();
  const rect = board.getBoundingClientRect();
  const x = Math.floor((event.clientX - rect.left) / CELL);
  const y = Math.floor((event.clientY - rect.top) / CELL);
  if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= COLS || y >= ROWS) return;

  const stampId = Number(event.dataTransfer.getData("application/x-stamp-id"));
  if (stampId) {
    const stamp = stamps.find((item) => item.id === stampId);
    if (!stamp) return;
    const asset = assetById(stamp.assetId);
    if (!asset) return;
    const origin = clampOrigin(asset, x, y);
    const removed = removeOverlappingStamps({
      x: origin.x,
      y: origin.y,
      w: Math.max(1, stamp.w),
      h: Math.max(1, stamp.h),
    }, stamp.id);
    stamp.x = origin.x;
    stamp.y = origin.y;
    selectedStampId = stamp.id;
    renderStamps();
    statusText.textContent = removed > 0
      ? `已移动 ${asset.name}，并替换 ${removed} 个上层素材：${origin.x}, ${origin.y}`
      : `已移动 ${asset.name}：${origin.x}, ${origin.y}`;
    return;
  }

  const asset = assetById(event.dataTransfer.getData("text/plain"));
  if (asset) placeAsset(asset, x, y);
});

function fillDefaultTerrain() {
  const asset = defaultTile();
  for (let y = 0; y < ROWS; y += 1) {
    for (let x = 0; x < COLS; x += 1) {
      terrain[y][x] = asset?.id || null;
    }
  }
  renderTerrain();
  statusText.textContent = `已将地图版面铺满 ${asset?.name || "默认地块"}。`;
}

function clearMap() {
  fillDefaultTerrain();
  stamps.length = 0;
  selectedStampId = null;
  renderStamps();
  statusText.textContent = "已清除之前的地图内容。";
}

function normalizeTerrainCell(cell) {
  if (typeof cell === "string") return cell;
  if (cell && typeof cell === "object") return cell.assetId || null;
  return null;
}

function applyMapData(data, label = <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1432" />ener("click", exportMap);
  document.getElementById("exportGodotMap").addEventListener("click", exportGodotMap);
  document.getElementById("playGodotMap").addEventListener("click", playGodotMap);
  assetSearch.addEventListener("input", renderAssetList);
  packageSelect.addEventListener("change", () => {
    activePackage = packageSelect.value;
    activeCategory = "全部";
    buildCategories();
    renderAssetList();
  });

  window.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
    if (event.key === "Delete" && selectedStampId != null) {
      const index = stamps.findIndex((stamp) => stamp.id === selectedStampId);
      if (index >= 0) {
        const asset = assetById(stamps[index].assetId);
        stamps.splice(index, 1);
        selectedStampId = null;
        renderStamps();
        statusText.textContent = `已删除 ${asset.name}。`;
      }
    }
    if (event.key === "Escape") {
      selectedStampId = null;
      renderStamps();
      statusText.textContent = "已取消选择。";
    }
  });
}

async function loadLibrary() {
  const response = await fetch(MANIFEST_URL);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`无法读取 Kenney manifest: ${response.status}`);
  const manifest = await response.json();
  ASSETS = tinyOnlyAssets(manifest.assets || []);
  PACKAGES = tinyOnlyPackages(manifest.packages || [], ASSETS);
  if (!PACKAGES.some((pack) => pack.name === activePackage)) activePackage = PACKAGES[0]?.name || "全部";
  assetLibraryMeta.textContent = `Tiny、AI tiny 与收藏素材 ${ASSETS.length} 个 / ${PACKAGES.length} 个素材包`;
  selectedAsset = defaultTile();
}

async function start() {
  bindEvents();
  buildBoard();
  await loadLibrary();
  buildPackageSelect();
  buildCategories();
  fillDefaultTerrain();
  renderAssetList();
  if (selectedAsset) selectAsset(selectedAsset);
  await loadDemoMap();
}

start().catch((error) => {
  console.error(error);
  statusText.textContent = `素材库加载失败：${error.message}`;
});

# Browser

Use this skill for browser automation tasks such as inspecting pages, navigating, testing local apps, clicking, typing, taking screenshots, and reading visible page state. After setup, select the `iab` browser.

Keep browser work in the background by default.

Show the browser when the user's request is primarily to put a page in front of them or let them watch the interaction, such as "open localhost:3000", "go to the docs page", "take me to the PR", "show me the current tab", or "keep the browser open while you test checkout".

Do not show the browser when navigation is only a means to answer a question or verify behavior, such as "check localhost:3000 and tell me whether login works", "inspect the docs page and summarize what changed", or "verify the modal still opens correctly". Localhost targets and ordinary page navigation do not by themselves require visibility.

When the browser should be visible to the user, actually present it with `await (await browser.capabilities.get("visibility")).set(true)`.

If this plugin is listed as available in the session, treat that as mandatory reading before browser work. Open and follow this skill before saying that Browser is unavailable and before falling back to standalone Playwright or Computer Use.

Do not skip this skill just because Computer Use MCP tool calls are directly visible or appear easier to invoke. The presence of Computer Use tools is not evidence that Computer Use is the preferred browser surface.

Before using this skill for the first time in the current conversation context, read<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2463" />cell that never created the binding.

### General guidance

* Minimize interruptions as much as possible. Only ask clarifying questions if you really need to. If a user has an under-specified prompt, try to fulfill it first before asking for more information.
* Remember, the user is asking questions about what they see on the screen. Base your interactions on what is visible to the user (based on DOM and screenshots) rather than programmatically determining what they are talking about. The "first link" on the page is not necessarily the first `a href` in the DOM.
* Try not to over-complicate things. It is okay to click based on node ID if it is not clear how to determine the UI element in Playwright.
* If a tab is already on a given URL, do not call `goto` with the same URL. This will reload the page and may lose any in-progress information the user has provided. When you intentionally need to reload, call `tab.reload()`.
* If browser-use is interrupted because the extension or user took control, do not quote the raw runtime error. Summarize it naturally for the user, for example: "Browser use was stopped in the extension." Avoid internal terms like turn_id, runtime, retry, or plugin error text unless the user asks for details.
* When testing a user's local app on `localhost`, `127.0.0.1`, `::1`, or another local development URL in a framework that does not support hot reloading or hot reloading is disabled, call `tab.reload()` after code or build changes before verifying the UI. After reloading, take a fresh DOM snapshot or screenshot before continuing.
* Do not brute-force undocumented site search URLs, query parameter variants, search engine query grids, or candidate URL arrays unless the user explicitly asks for exhaustive coverage.
* If a guessed URL, search query, or candidate page fails, try at most one new approach. After that, switch to visible page navigation, the site's own search UI, or give the best current answer with uncertainty.

[54] user: 样板森林边缘：按 Sample 左侧森林的单株树密排方式。
样板混合树组：按 Sample 中散树/小树林混排方式。
红顶蓝墙房屋：按 Sample 左上房屋组合。
灰顶木墙房屋：按 Sample 中部/右侧小屋组合。
城堡门楼：按 Sample 右下城堡的塔楼 + 中央门楼复合结构。
泥地区块：按 Sample 右上泥地块组合。和原图不同，需要每个板块和原图一致

function buildBoard() {
  board.innerHTML = "";
  cells.length = 0;
  for (let y = 0; y < ROWS; y += 1) {
    for (let x = 0; x < COLS; x += 1) {
      const cell = document.createElement("button");
      cell.type = "button";
      cell.className = "grid-cell";
      cell.dataset.x = String(x);
      cell.dataset.y = String(y);
      cell.style.left = `${x * CELL}px`;
      cell.style.top = `${y * CELL}px`;
      cell.title = `${x}, ${y}`;
      cell.addEventListener("click", () => placeSelected(x, y));
      cell.addEventListener("dragover", (event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        cell.classList.add("drop-target");
      });
      cell.addEventListener("dragleave", () => cell.classList.remove("drop-target"));
      cell.addEventListener("drop", (event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        cell.classList.remove("drop-target");
        const asset = assetById(event.dataTransfer.getData("text/plain"));
        if (asset) placeAsset(asset, x, y);
      });
      board.appendChild(cell);
      cells.push(cell);
    }
  }
}

function renderTerrain() {
  for (const cell of cells) {
    const x = Number(cell.dataset.x);
    const y = Number(cell.dataset.y);
    const asset = assetById(terrain[y][x]) || defaultTile();
    cell.style.backgroundImage = asset ? `url("${assetUrl(asset)}")` : "";
  }
}

function buildPackageSelect() {
  packageSelect.innerHTML = "";
  const all = document.createElement("option");
  all.value = "全部";
  all.textContent = `全部素材包 (${ASSETS.length})`;
  packageSelect.appendChild(all);

  for (const pack of PACKAGES) {
    const option = document.createElement("option");
    option.value = pack.name;
    option.textContent = `${pack.name} (${pack.count})`;
    packageSelect.appendChild(option);
  }
  packageSelect.value = activePackage;

func _puny_frame(coord: Vector2i) -> AtlasTexture:
	return _atlas_custom(CHARACTER_SHEET, Rect2(coord.x * 32, coord.y * 32, 32, 32))

func _update_character_animation(direction: Vector2) -> void:
	if player_sprite == null:
		return
	if direction == Vector2.ZERO:
		player_sprite.play("idle_%s" % player_facing)
		return
	if abs(direction.x) > abs(direction.y):
		player_facing = "right" if direction.x > 0 else "left"
	else:
		player_facing = "down" if direction.y > 0 else "up"
	player_sprite.play("walk_%s" % player_facing)

func _add_game_ui() -> void:

func _atlas_custom(path: String, region: Rect2) -> AtlasTexture:
	var key := "%s:%s" % [path, region]
	if atlas_cache.has(key):
		return atlas_cache[key]
	var tex := AtlasTexture.new()
	tex.atlas = _texture(path)
	tex.region = region
	tex.filter_clip = true
	atlas_cache[key] = tex
	return tex

func _texture(path: String) -> Texture2D:
	if atlas_cache.has(path):
		return atlas_cache[path]
	var image := Image.new()
	var err := image.load(ProjectSettings.globalize_path(path))
	var texture: Texture2D = null
	if err == OK:
		_normalize_texture_alpha(path, image)
		texture = ImageTexture.create_from_image(image)
	else:
		texture = load(path) as Texture2D
		if texture == null:
			push_error("Missing texture: " + path)
		elif _needs_edge_alpha_cleanup(path):
			var imported_image := texture.get_image()

function downloadJson(data, filename) {
  const blob = new Blob([`${JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)}\n`], { type: "application/json" });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const link = document.createElement("a");
  link.href = url;
  link.download = filename;
  link.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

function exportMap() {
  copyJson(currentEditorMapData(), "地图编辑器 JSON 已复制到剪贴板。");
}

function exportGodotMap() {
  const data = godotMapData();
  downloadJson(data, "gamegmeta_godot_map.json");
  copyJson(data, "Godot 地图 JSON 已下载，并复制到剪贴板。");
}

function playGodotMap() {
  const data = godotMapData();
  const serialized = JSON.stringify(data);
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(GODOT_EDITOR_MAP_STORAGE_KEY, serialized);
  } catch (_error) {
    // Some embedded browser contexts restrict localStorage; window.name survives same-tab navigation.
  }
  try {
    window.name = serialized;
  } catch (_error) {
    // URL hash below remains the primary no-server handoff path.
  }
  const encodedMap = encodeURIComponent(serialized);
  statusText.textContent = `已发送当前地图到 Godot：${data.objects.length} 个上层素材。`;
  window.location.href = `../exports/web/index.html?map=editor&build=${Date.now()}#map=${encodedMap}`;
}

function bindEvents() {
  document.getElementById("loadDemo").addEventListener("click", () => {
    loadDemoMap().catch((error) => {
      console.error(error);
      statusText.textContent = `Demo 加载失败：${error.message}`;
    });
  });
  document.getElementById("fillGrass").addEventListener("click", fillDefaultTerrain);
  document.getElementById("clearMap").addEventListener("click", clearMap);

func _ready() -> void:
	RenderingServer.set_default_clear_color(Color("#82c96a"))
	get_viewport().canvas_item_default_texture_filter = Viewport.DEFAULT_CANVAS_ITEM_TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST
	runtime_tile_set = _create_runtime_tileset()
	_load_map(0)

func _physics_process(_delta: float) -> void:
	if player == null or not player.visible:
		return
	var direction := Input.get_vector("move_left", "move_right", "move_up", "move_down")
	player.velocity = direction * PLAYER_SPEED
	player.move_and_slide()
	_update_character_animation(direction)

func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event.is_action_pressed("ui_cancel") and player != null:
		_load_map(0)

func _input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if ui_layer == null or ui_layer.name != "MapCardMenu":
		return
	if event is InputEventMouseButton and event.pressed and event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT:
		var chosen := _card_index_at(event.position)
		if chosen >= 0:
			_load_map(chosen)
	if event is InputEventKey and event.pressed:
		var key_index: int = event.keycode - KEY_1
		if key_index >= 0 and key_index < map_cards.size():
			_load_map(key_index)

func _card_index_at(pos: Vector2) -> int:
	var card_w := 224
	var card_h := 252
	var gap := 18
	for i in map_cards.size():
		var rect := Rect2(Vector2(48 + i * (card_w + gap), 142), Vector2(card_w, card_h))
		if rect.has_point(pos):
			return i
	return -1

	bg.size = Vector2(1280, 720)
	ui_layer.add_child(bg)

	var title := Label.new()
	title.text = "GameGmeta TileSet Maps"

func _load_map(index: int) -> void:
	current_map = map_cards[index]
	_clear_scene()
	active_map_w = MAP_W
	active_map_h = MAP_H
	active_cell_px = TILE
	map_root = Node2D.new()

	var decor := _create_tile_layer("DecorTileMapLayer", 4)

	match current_map.id:
		MAP_CITY:
			_build_city_map(ground, route, object, decor)
		MAP_VILLAGE:

		MAP_WORLD:

		MAP_SCIFI:

		MAP_TOWER:

		MAP_EDITOR_JSON:
			_build_editor_json_map()

	_add_static_collision_cells(active_blocked_cells)
	_add_player(current_map.start)
	_add_game_ui()

	for cell in blocked.keys():
		var body := StaticBody2D.new()
		body.collision_layer = 1
		body.collision_mask = 1
		body.position = Vector2(cell.x * active_cell_px + active_cell_px * 0.5, cell.y * active_cell_px + active_cell_px * 0.5)
		var shape := CollisionShape2D.new()
		var rect := RectangleShape2D.new()
		rect.size = Vector2(active_cell_px, active_cell_px)
		shape.shape = rect
		body.add_child(shape)
		root.add_child(body)

func _add_player(tile_position: Vector2i) -> void:
	player = CharacterBody2D.new()
	player.name = "Player_CharacterBody2D"
	var clamped_tile := Vector2i(
		clamp(tile_position.x, 1, max(1, active_map_w - 2)),
		clamp(tile_position.y, 1, max(1, active_map_h - 2))
	)
	player.position = Vector2(clamped_tile.x * active_cell_px + active_cell_px * 0.5, clamped_tile.y * active_cell_px + active_cell_px * 0.5)
	player.collision_layer = 1
	player.collision_mask = 1
	player.z_index = 20
	map_root.add_child(player)

	var collision := CollisionShape2D.new()
	var circle := CircleShape2D.new()
	circle.radius = 10
	collision.shape = circle
	player.add_child(collision)

	player_sprite = AnimatedSprite2D.new()
	player_sprite.name = "AnimatedPunyWarrior"
	player_sprite.sprite_frames = _create_character_frames()
	player_sprite.animation = "idle_down"
	player_sprite.scale = Vector2(PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE, PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE)
	player_sprite.texture_filter = CanvasItem.TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST
	player.add_child(player_sprite)

	camera = Camera2D.new()
	camera.name = "FollowCamera"
	camera.zoom = Vector2.ONE if current_map.id == MAP_EDITOR_JSON else Vector2(1.6, 1.6)
	camera.position_smoothing_enabled = true
	camera.position_smoothing_speed = 7.5
	camera.limit_left = 0
	camera.limit_top = 0
	camera.limit_right = active_map_w * active_cell_px
	camera.limit_bottom = active_map_h * active_cell_px
	player.add_child(camera)
	camera.make_current()

func _create_character_frames() -> SpriteFrames:
	var frames := SpriteFrames.new()
	for anim in ["idle_down", "idle_up", "idle_right", "idle_left", "walk_down", "walk_up", "walk_right", "walk_left"]:
		frames.add_animation(anim)
		frames.set_animation_speed(anim, 8.0)

function savedMaps() {
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SAVED_MAPS_STORAGE_KEY) || "[]");
    if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) return parsed;
    for (const key of LEGACY_SAVED_MAPS_STORAGE_KEYS) {
      const legacy = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) || "[]");
      if (Array.isArray(legacy) && legacy.length > 0) return legacy;
    }
    return [];
  } catch (_error) {
    return [];
  }
}

function writeSavedMaps(maps) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(SAVED_MAPS_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(maps));
    return true;
  } catch (_error) {
    return false;
  }
}

function mapTimestampLabel(date = new Date()) {
  const pad = (value) => String(value).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())} ${pad(date.getHours())}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}`;
}

function renderSavedMaps(selectedId = savedMapSelect.value) {
  const maps = savedMaps().sort((a, b) => String(b.updatedAt || "").localeCompare(String(a.updatedAt || "")));
  savedMapSelect.innerHTML = "";
  if (maps.length === 0) {
    const option = document.createElement("option");
    option.value = "";
    option.textContent = "暂无保存地图";
    savedMapSelect.appendChild(option);
    return;
  }

  for (const item of maps) {
    const option = document.createElement("option");
    option.value = item.id;
    option.textContent = item.name;
    savedMapSelect.appendChild(option);
  }
  if (selectedId && maps.some((item) => item.id === selectedId)) {
    savedMapSelect.value = selectedId;
  }
}

function currentSaveName() {
  const typedName = mapNameInput.value.trim();
  if (typedName) return typedName;
  if (currentMapName && currentMapName !== "Demo") return currentMapName;
  return `未命名地图 ${mapTimestampLabel()}`;
}

function saveCurrentMap() {
  const name = currentSaveName();
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const data = { ...currentEditorMapData(), name };
  const maps = savedMaps();
  const existingIndex = maps.findIndex((item) => item.name === name);
  const existingId = existingIndex >= 0 ? maps[existingIndex].id : null;
  const record = {
    id: existingId || `map-${Date.now()}`,
    name,
    updatedAt: now,
    stampCount: stamps.length,
    data,
  };
  if (existingIndex >= 0) {
    maps[existingIndex] = record;
  } else {
    maps.push(record);
  }
  if (!writeSavedMaps(maps)) {
    statusText.textContent = "保存失败：当前浏览器不允许写入本地存储。请使用 http://127.0.0.1:8794/web/index.html 打开编辑器。";
    return;
  }
  currentMapName = name;
  mapNameInput.value = name;
  renderSavedMaps(record.id);
  statusText.textContent = `已保存地图「${name}」。每个不同名称会成为“我的地图”中的一个新地图。`;
}

function openSavedMap() {
  const id = savedMapSelect.value;
  const record = savedMaps().find((item) => item.id === id);

function deleteSavedMap() {
  const id = savedMapSelect.value;
  const record = savedMaps().find((item) => item.id === id);
  if (!record) {
    statusText.textContent = "没有可删除的保存地图。";
    return;
  }
  const maps = savedMaps().filter((item) => item.id !== id);
  if (!writeSavedMaps(maps)) {
    statusText.textContent = "删除失败：当前浏览器不允许写入本地存储。";
    return;
  }
  renderSavedMaps();
  statusText.textContent = `已删除保存地图「${record.name}」。`;
}

function stampTexturePath(stamp) {
  const asset = assetById(stamp.assetId);
  return asset?.file ? `res://${asset.file}` : "";
}

function godotMapData() {
  const groundId = defaultTile()?.id || GROUND_ASSET_ID;
  return {
    format: "GameGmetaGodotMap",
    version: 1,
    cols: COLS,
    rows: ROWS,
    cell: CELL,
    terrain: terrain.map((row) => row.map(() => {
      const asset = assetById(groundId);
      return {
        assetId: groundId,
        texture: asset?.file ? `res://${asset.file}` : "",
        blocking: false,
      };
    })),
    objects: stamps.map((stamp) => ({
      id: stamp.id,
      assetId: stamp.assetId || null,
      texture: stampTexturePath(stamp),
      x: stamp.x,
      y: stamp.y,
      w: stamp.w,
      h: stamp.h,
      blocking: Boolean(stamp.blocking),
      z: 6,
    })),
  };
}

function isBlockingAsset(asset) {
  if (!asset || asset.id === GROUND_ASSET_ID) return false;
  if (asset.mode !== "tile") return true;

灰顶木墙房屋：按 Sample 中部/右侧小屋组合。
城堡门楼：按 Sample 右下城堡的塔楼 + 中央门楼复合结构。

  <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1231" />asset.mode === "tile" && /grass|terrain|green/i.test(`${asset.id} ${asset.name}`)) ||

function buildBoard() {
  board.innerHTML = "";
  cells.length = 0;
  for (let y = 0; y < ROWS; y += 1) {
    for (let x = 0; x < COLS; x += 1) {
      const cell = document.createElement("button");
      cell.type = "button";
      cell.className = "grid-cell";
      cell.dataset.x = String(x);
      cell.dataset.y = String(y);
      cell.style.left = `${x * CELL}px`;
      cell.style.top = `${y * CELL}px`;
      cell.title = `${x}, ${y}`;
      cell.addEventListener("click", () => placeSelected(x, y));
      cell.addEventListener("dragover", (event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        cell.classList.add("drop-target");
      });
      cell.addEventListener("dragleave", () => cell.classList.remove("drop-target"));
      cell.addEventListener("drop", (event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        cell.classList.remove("drop-target");
        const asset = assetById(event.dataTransfer.getData("text/plain"));
        if (asset) placeAsset(asset, x, y);
      });
      board.appendChild(cell);
      cells.push(cell);
    }
  }
}

function renderTerrain() {
  for (const cell of cells) {
    const x = Number(cell.dataset.x);
    const y = Number(cell.dataset.y);
    const asset = assetById(terrain[y][x]) || defaultTile();
    cell.style.backgroundImage = asset ? `url("${assetUrl(asset)}")` : "";
  }
}

function buildPackageSelect() {
  packageSelect.innerHTML = "";
  const all = document.createElement("option");
  all.value = "全部";
  all.textContent = `全部素材包 (${ASSETS.length})`;
  packageSelect.appendChild(all);

  for (const pack of PACKAGES) {
    const option = document.createElement("option");
    option.value = pack.name;
    option.textContent = `${pack.name} (${pack.count})`;
    packageSelect.appendChild(option);
  }
  packageSelect.value = activePackage;

func _puny_frame(coord: Vector2i) -> AtlasTexture:
	return _atlas_custom(CHARACTER_SHEET, Rect2(coord.x * 32, coord.y * 32, 32, 32))

func _update_character_animation(direction: Vector2) -> void:
	if player_sprite == null:
		return
	if direction == Vector2.ZERO:
		player_sprite.play("idle_%s" % player_facing)
		return
	if abs(direction.x) > abs(direction.y):
		player_facing = "right" if direction.x > 0 else "left"
	else:
		player_facing = "down" if direction.y > 0 else "up"
	player_sprite.play("walk_%s" % player_facing)

func _add_game_ui() -> void:
	ui_layer = CanvasLayer.new()
	ui_layer.name = "MapRuntimeUI"
	add_child(ui_layer)

	var panel := ColorRect.new()
	panel.color = Color(0.05, 0.06, 0.07, 0.88)

func _atlas_custom(path: String, region: Rect2) -> AtlasTexture:
	var key := "%s:%s" % [path, region]
	if atlas_cache.has(key):
		return atlas_cache[key]
	var tex := AtlasTexture.new()
	tex.atlas = _texture(path)
	tex.region = region
	tex.filter_clip = true
	atlas_cache[key] = tex
	return tex

func _texture(path: String) -> Texture2D:
	if atlas_cache.has(path):
		return atlas_cache[path]
	var image := Image.new()
	var err := image.load(ProjectSettings.globalize_path(path))
	var texture: Texture2D = null
	if err == OK:
		_normalize_texture_alpha(path, image)
		texture = ImageTexture.create_from_image(image)
	else:
		texture = load(path) as Texture2D
		if texture == null:
			push_error("Missing texture: " + path)
		elif _needs_edge_alpha_cleanup(path):
			var imported_image := texture.get_image()

function downloadJson(data, filename) {
  const blob = new Blob([`${JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)}\n`], { type: "application/json" });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const link = document.createElement("a");
  link.href = url;
  link.download = filename;
  link.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

function exportMap() {
  copyJson(currentEditorMapData(), "地图编辑器 JSON 已复制到剪贴板。");
}

function exportGodotMap() {
  const data = godotMapData();
  downloadJson(data, "gamegmeta_godot_map.json");
  copyJson(data, "Godot 地图 JSON 已下载，并复制到剪贴板。");
}

function playGodotMap() {
  const data = godotMapData();
  const serialized = JSON.stringify(data);
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(GODOT_EDITOR_MAP_STORAGE_KEY, serialized);
  } catch (_error) {
    // Some embedded browser contexts restrict localStorage; window.name survives same-tab navigation.
  }
  try {
    window.name = serialized;
  } catch (_error) {
    // URL hash below remains the primary no-server handoff path.
  }
  const encodedMap = encodeURIComponent(serialized);
  statusText.textContent = `已发送当前地图到 Godot：${data.objects.length} 个上层素材。`;
  window.location.href = `../exports/web/index.html?map=editor&build=${Date.now()}#map=${encodedMap}`;
}

function bindEvents() {
  document.getElementById("loadDemo").addEventListener("click", () => {
    loadDemoMap().catch((error) => {
      console.error(error);
      statusText.textContent = `Demo 加载失败：${error.message}`;
    });
  });
  document.getElementById("fillGrass").addEventListener("click", fillDefaultTerrain);
  document.getElementById("clearMap").addEventListener("click", clearMap);

const T_TARGET := Vector2i(11, 7)

const S_FACTORY := Vector2i(14, 1)
const S_DOME := Vector2i(15, 1)
const S_HANGAR := Vector2i(16, 0)
const S_REFINERY := Vector2i(17, 1)
const S_POWER := Vector2i(15, 5)

const S_PURPLE_TREE := Vector2i(1, 2)

const S_ROCK := Vector2i(3, 3)

const MAP_CITY := "city"
const MAP_VILLAGE := "village"
const MAP_WORLD := "world"
const MAP_SCIFI := "scifi"
const MAP_TOWER := "tower"
const MAP_EDITOR_JSON := "editor_json"

var map_cards := [
	{"id": MAP_EDITOR_JSON, "title": "Kenney JSON Map", "subtitle": "Generated from web editor JSON with local Kenney PNG assets", "preview": "res://assets/game/kenney-map-library/tiny-town/Tiles/tile_0000.png", "start": Vector2i(16, 18)},

var atlas_cache: Dictionary = {}

var map_root: Node2D
var ui_layer: CanvasLayer
var player: CharacterBody2D
var player_sprite: AnimatedSprite2D
var camera: Camera2D
var current_map: Dictionary = {}
var player_facing := "down"
var active_blocked_cells: Dictionary = {}
var active_map_w := MAP_W
var active_map_h := MAP_H
var active_cell_px := TILE

func _ready() -> void:
	RenderingServer.set_default_clear_color(Color("#82c96a"))
	get_viewport().canvas_item_default_texture_filter = Viewport.DEFAULT_CANVAS_ITEM_TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST
	runtime_tile_set = _create_runtime_tileset()
	_load_map(0)

func _physics_process(_delta: float) -> void:
	if player == null or not player.visible:
		return
	var direction := Input.get_vector("move_left", "move_right", "move_up", "move_down")
	player.velocity = direction * PLAYER_SPEED
	player.move_and_slide()
	_update_character_animation(direction)

func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event.is_action_pressed("ui_cancel") and player != null:
		_load_map(0)

func _input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if ui_layer == null or ui_layer.name != "MapCardMenu":
		return
	if event is InputEventMouseButton and event.pressed and event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT:
		var chosen := _card_index_at(event.position)
		if chosen >= 0:
			_load_map(chosen)
	if event is InputEventKey and event.pressed:
		var key_index: int = event.keycode - KEY_1
		if key_index >= 0 and key_index < map_cards.size():
			_load_map(key_index)

func _card_index_at(pos: Vector2) -> int:
	var card_w := 224
	var card_h := 252
	var gap := 18
	for i in map_cards.size():
		var rect := Rect2(Vector2(48 + i * (card_w + gap), 142), Vector2(card_w, card_h))
		if rect.has_point(pos):
			return i
	return -1

func _show_map_cards() -> void:
	_clear_scene()
	ui_layer = CanvasLayer.new()
	ui_layer.name = "MapCardMenu"
	add_child(ui_layer)

	var bg := ColorRect.new()
	bg.color = Color(0.08, 0.1, 0.12)
	bg.size = Vector2(1280, 720)
	ui_layer.add_child(bg)

	var title := Label.new()
	title.text = "GameGmeta TileSet Maps"

func _load_map(index: int) -> void:
	current_map = map_cards[index]
	_clear_scene()
	active_map_w = MAP_W
	active_map_h = MAP_H
	active_cell_px = TILE
	map_root = Node2D.new()
	map_root.name = "TileMapScene_%s" % current_map.id
	add_child(map_root)
	active_blocked_cells.clear()

	var ground := _create_tile_layer("GroundTileMapLayer", 0)
	var route := _create_tile_layer("RouteTileMapLayer", 1)
	var object := _create_tile_layer("WallWaterBuildingPhysicsTileMapLayer", 3)
	var decor := _create_tile_layer("DecorTileMapLayer", 4)

	match current_map.id:
		MAP_CITY:
			_build_city_map(ground, route, object, decor)
		MAP_VILLAGE:
			_build_village_map(ground, route, object, decor)
		MAP_WORLD:
			_build_world_map(ground, route, object, decor)
		MAP_SCIFI:
			_build_scifi_map(ground, route, object, decor)
		MAP_TOWER:
			_build_tower_map(ground, route, object, decor)
		MAP_EDITOR_JSON:
			_build_editor_json_map()

	_add_static_collision_cells(active_blocked_cells)
	_add_player(current_map.start)
	_add_game_ui()

func _clear_scene() -> void:
	if map_root:
		map_root.queue_free()
		map_root = null
	if ui_layer:
		ui_layer.queue_free()
		ui_layer = null
	player = null
	player_sprite = null

func _create_runtime_tileset() -> TileSet:
	var set := TileSet.new()
	set.tile_size = Vector2i(TILE, TILE)
	set.add_physics_layer()

func _add_static_collision_cells(blocked: Dictionary) -> void:
	var root := Node2D.new()
	root.name = "GeneratedPhysicsBoundaries_StaticBody2D"
	root.visible = false
	map_root.add_child(root)
	for cell in blocked.keys():
		var body := StaticBody2D.new()
		body.collision_layer = 1
		body.collision_mask = 1
		body.position = Vector2(cell.x * active_cell_px + active_cell_px * 0.5, cell.y * active_cell_px + active_cell_px * 0.5)
		var shape := CollisionShape2D.new()
		var rect := RectangleShape2D.new()
		rect.size = Vector2(active_cell_px, active_cell_px)
		shape.shape = rect
		body.add_child(shape)
		root.add_child(body)

func _add_player(tile_position: Vector2i) -> void:
	player = CharacterBody2D.new()
	player.name = "Player_CharacterBody2D"
	var clamped_tile := Vector2i(
		clamp(tile_position.x, 1, max(1, active_map_w - 2)),
		clamp(tile_position.y, 1, max(1, active_map_h - 2))
	)
	player.position = Vector2(clamped_tile.x * active_cell_px + active_cell_px * 0.5, clamped_tile.y * active_cell_px + active_cell_px * 0.5)
	player.collision_layer = 1
	player.collision_mask = 1
	player.z_index = 20
	map_root.add_child(player)

	var collision := CollisionShape2D.new()
	var circle := CircleShape2D.new()
	circle.radius = 10
	collision.shape = circle
	player.add_child(collision)

	player_sprite = AnimatedSprite2D.new()
	player_sprite.name = "AnimatedPunyWarrior"
	player_sprite.sprite_frames = _create_character_frames()
	player_sprite.animation = "idle_down"
	player_sprite.scale = Vector2(PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE, PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE)
	player_sprite.texture_filter = CanvasItem.TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST
	player.add_child(player_sprite)

	camera = Camera2D.new()
	camera.name = "FollowCamera"
	camera.zoom = Vector2.ONE if current_map.id == MAP_EDITOR_JSON else Vector2(1.6, 1.6)
	camera.position_smoothing_enabled = true
	camera.position_smoothing_speed = 7.5
	camera.limit_left = 0
	camera.limit_top = 0
	camera.limit_right = active_map_w * active_cell_px
	camera.limit_bottom = active_map_h * active_cell_px
	player.add_child(camera)
	camera.make_current()

func _create_character_frames() -> SpriteFrames:
	var frames := SpriteFrames.new()
	for anim in ["idle_down", "idle_up", "idle_right", "idle_left", "walk_down", "walk_up", "walk_right", "walk_left"]:
		frames.add_animation(anim)
		frames.set_animation_speed(anim, 8.0)

}

function savedMaps() {
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SAVED_MAPS_STORAGE_KEY) || "[]");
    if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) return parsed;
    for (const key of LEGACY_SAVED_MAPS_STORAGE_KEYS) {
      const legacy = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) || "[]");
      if (Array.isArray(legacy) && legacy.length > 0) return legacy;
    }
    return [];
  } catch (_error) {
    return [];
  }
}

function writeSavedMaps(maps) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(SAVED_MAPS_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(maps));
    return true;
  } catch (_error) {
    return false;
  }
}

function mapTimestampLabel(date = new Date()) {
  const pad = (value) => String(value).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())} ${pad(date.getHours())}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}`;
}

function renderSavedMaps(selectedId = savedMapSelect.value) {
  const maps = savedMaps().sort((a, b) => String(b.updatedAt || "").localeCompare(String(a.updatedAt || "")));
  savedMapSelect.innerHTML = "";
  if (maps.length === 0) {
    const option = document.createElement("option");
    option.value = "";
    option.textContent = "暂无保存地图";
    savedMapSelect.appendChild(option);
    return;
  }

  for (const item of maps) {
    const option = document.createElement("option");
    option.value = item.id;
    option.textContent = item.name;
    savedMapSelect.appendChild(option);
  }
  if (selectedId && maps.some((item) => item.id === selectedId)) {
    savedMapSelect.value = selectedId;
  }
}

function currentSaveName() {
  const typedName = mapNameInput.value.trim();
  if (typedName) return typedName;
  if (currentMapName && currentMapName !== "Demo") return currentMapName;
  return `未命名地图 ${mapTimestampLabel()}`;
}

function saveCurrentMap() {
  const name = currentSaveName();
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const data = { ...currentEditorMapData(), name };
  const maps = savedMaps();
  const existingIndex = maps.findIndex((item) => item.name === name);
  const existingId = existingIndex >= 0 ? maps[existingIndex].id : null;
  const record = {
    id: existingId || `map-${Date.now()}`,
    name,
    updatedAt: now,
    stampCount: stamps.length,
    data,
  };
  if (existingIndex >= 0) {
    maps[existingIndex] = record;
  } else {
    maps.push(record);
  }
  if (!writeSavedMaps(maps)) {
    statusText.textContent = "保存失败：当前浏览器不允许写入本地存储。请使用 http://127.0.0.1:8794/web/index.html 打开编辑器。";
    return;
  }
  currentMapName = name;
  mapNameInput.value = name;
  renderSavedMaps(record.id);
  statusText.textContent = `已保存地图「${name}」。每个不同名称会成为“我的地图”中的一个新地图。`;
}

function openSavedMap() {
  const id = savedMapSelect.value;
  const record = savedMaps().find((item) => item.id === id);

function deleteSavedMap() {
  const id = savedMapSelect.value;
  const record = savedMaps().find((item) => item.id === id);
  if (!record) {
    statusText.textContent = "没有可删除的保存地图。";
    return;
  }
  const maps = savedMaps().filter((item) => item.id !== id);
  if (!writeSavedMaps(maps)) {
    statusText.textContent = "删除失败：当前浏览器不允许写入本地存储。";
    return;
  }
  renderSavedMaps();
  statusText.textContent = `已删除保存地图「${record.name}」。`;
}

function stampTexturePath(stamp) {
  const asset = assetById(stamp.assetId);
  return asset?.file ? `res://${asset.file}` : "";
}

function godotMapData() {
  const groundId = defaultTile()?.id || GROUND_ASSET_ID;
  return {
    format: "GameGmetaGodotMap",
    version: 1,
    cols: COLS,
    rows: ROWS,
    cell: CELL,
    terrain: terrain.map((row) => row.map(() => {
      const asset = assetById(groundId);
      return {
        assetId: groundId,
        texture: asset?.file ? `res://${asset.file}` : "",
        blocking: false,
      };
    })),
    objects: stamps.map((stamp) => ({
      id: stamp.id,
      assetId: stamp.assetId || null,
      texture: stampTexturePath(stamp),
      x: stamp.x,
      y: stamp.y,
      w: stamp.w,
      h: stamp.h,
      blocking: Boolean(stamp.blocking),
      z: 6,
    })),
  };
}

function isBlockingAsset(asset) {

    "tiny-town/Tiles/tile_0002",
    "tiny-town/Tiles/tile_0012",
    "tiny-town/Tiles/tile_0013",
    "tiny-town/Tiles/tile_0014",

function savedMaps() {
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SAVED_MAPS_STORAGE_KEY) || "[]");
    if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) return parsed;
    for (const key of LEGACY_SAVED_MAPS_STORAGE_KEYS) {
      const legacy = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) || "[]");
      if (Array.isArray(legacy) && legacy.length > 0) return legacy;
    }
    return [];
  } catch (_error) {
    return [];
  }
}

function writeSavedMaps(maps) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(SAVED_MAPS_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(maps));
    return true;
  } catch (_error) {
    return false;
  }
}

function mapTimestampLabel(date = new Date()) {
  const pad = (value) => String(value).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())} ${pad(date.getHours())}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}`;
}

function renderSavedMaps(selectedId = savedMapSelect.value) {
  const maps = savedMaps().sort((a, b) => String(b.updatedAt || "").localeCompare(String(a.updatedAt || "")));
  savedMapSelect.innerHTML = "";
  if (maps.length === 0) {
    const option = document.createElement("option");
    option.value = "";
    option.textContent = "暂无保存地图";
    savedMapSelect.appendChild(option);
    return;
  }

  for (const item of maps) {
    const option = document.createElement("option");
    option.value = item.id;
    option.textContent = item.name;
    savedMapSelect.appendChild(option);
  }
  if (selectedId && maps.some((item) => item.id === selectedId)) {
    savedMapSelect.value = selectedId;
  }
}

function currentSaveName() {
  const typedName = mapNameInput.value.trim();
  if (typedName) return typedName;
  if (currentMapName && currentMapName !== "Demo") return currentMapName;
  return `未命名地图 ${mapTimestampLabel()}`;
}

function saveCurrentMap() {
  const name = currentSaveName();
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const data = { ...currentEditorMapData(), name };
  const maps = savedMaps();
  const existingIndex = maps.findIndex((item) => item.name === name);
  const existingId = existingIndex >= 0 ? maps[existingIndex].id : null;
  const record = {
    id: existingId || `map-${Date.now()}`,
    name,
    updatedAt: now,
    stampCount: stamps.length,
    data,
  };
  if (existingIndex >= 0) {
    maps[existingIndex] = record;
  } else {
    maps.push(record);
  }
  if (!writeSavedMaps(maps)) {
    statusText.textContent = "保存失败：当前浏览器不允许写入本地存储。请使用 http://127.0.0.1:8794/web/index.html 打开编辑器。";
    return;
  }
  currentMapName = name;
  mapNameInput.value = name;
  renderSavedMaps(record.id);
  statusText.textContent = `已保存地图「${name}」。每个不同名称会成为“我的地图”中的一个新地图。`;
}

function openSavedMap() {
  const id = savedMapSelect.value;
  const record = savedMaps().find((item) => item.id === id);

function deleteSavedMap() {
  const id = savedMapSelect.value;
  const record = savedMaps().find((item) => item.id === id);
  if (!record) {
    statusText.textContent = "没有可删除的保存地图。";
    return;
  }
  const maps = savedMaps().filter((item) => item.id !== id);
  if (!writeSavedMaps(maps)) {
    statusText.textContent = "删除失败：当前浏览器不允许写入本地存储。";
    return;
  }
  renderSavedMaps();
  statusText.textContent = `已删除保存地图「${record.name}」。`;
}

function stampTexturePath(stamp) {
  const asset = assetById(stamp.assetId);
  return asset?.file ? `res://${asset.file}` : "";
}

function godotMapData() {
  const groundId = defaultTile()?.id || GROUND_ASSET_ID;
  return {
    format: "GameGmetaGodotMap",
    version: 1,
    cols: COLS,
    rows: ROWS,
    cell: CELL,
    terrain: terrain.map((row) => row.map(() => {
      const asset = assetById(groundId);
      return {
        assetId: groundId,
        texture: asset?.file ? `res://${asset.file}` : "",
        blocking: false,
      };
    })),
    objects: stamps.map((stamp) => ({
      id: stamp.id,
      assetId: stamp.assetId || null,
      texture: stampTexturePath(stamp),
      x: stamp.x,
      y: stamp.y,
      w: stamp.w,
      h: stamp.h,
      blocking: Boolean(stamp.blocking),
      z: 6,
    })),
  };
}

+function godotMapData() {
+  return godotMapDataFromEditorMap(currentEditorMapData());

+
+function godotRuntimeMaps(currentData) {
+  const maps = [{
+    id: "current-editor-map",
+    name: currentSaveName(),
+    data: currentData,
+  }];
+  for (const record of savedMaps()) {
+    if (!record?.data) continue;
+    maps.push({
+      id: record.id,
+      name: record.name,
+      data: godotMapDataFromEditorMap(record.data),
+    });
+  }
+  return { version: 1, maps };
+}
+
@@

 func _clear_scene() -> void:

+
+func _load_browser_runtime_maps() -> void:
+	if not OS.has_feature("web"):
+		return
+	var raw = JavaScriptBridge.eval("(function(){try{return localStorage.getItem('%s') || '';}catch(e){return '';}})()" % BROWSER_RUNTIME_MAPS_STORAGE_KEY, true)
+	if raw == null or String(raw).is_empty():
+		return
+	var parsed = JSON.parse_string(String(raw))
+	if typeof(parsed) != TYPE_DICTIONARY:
+		return
+	var saved_maps: Array = parsed.get("maps", [])
+	if saved_maps.is_empty():
+		return
+	map_cards.clear()
+	for i in saved_maps.size():
+		var entry = saved_maps[i]
+		if typeof(entry) != TYPE_DICTIONARY:
+			continue
+		var runtime_data = entry.get("data", {})
+		if typeof(runtime_data) != TYPE_DICTIONARY:
+			continue
+		var title := String(entry.get("name", "地图 %d" % [i + 1]))
+		map_cards.append({
+			"id": MAP_EDITOR_JSON,
+			"title": title,
+			"subtitle": "从地图编辑器保存的本地地图",
+			"preview": "res://assets/game/kenney-map-library/tiny-town/Tiles/tile_0000.png",
+			"start": Vector2i(2, 2),
+			"runtime_data": runtime_data,
+		})

[54] user: 样板森林边缘：按 Sample 左侧森林的单株树密排方式。
样板混合树组：按 Sample 中散树/小树林混排方式。
红顶蓝墙房屋：按 Sample 左上房屋组合。
灰顶木墙房屋：按 Sample 中部/右侧小屋组合。
城堡门楼：按 Sample 右下城堡的塔楼 + 中央门楼复合结构。
泥地区块：按 Sample 右上泥地块组合。和原图不同，需要每个板块和原图一致

function savedMaps() {
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SAVED_MAPS_STORAGE_KEY) || "[]");
    if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) return parsed;
    for (const key of LEGACY_SAVED_MAPS_STORAGE_KEYS) {
      const legacy = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) || "[]");
      if (Array.isArray(legacy) && legacy.length > 0) return legacy;
    }
    return [];
  } catch (_error) {
    return [];
  }
}

function writeSavedMaps(maps) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(SAVED_MAPS_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(maps));
    return true;
  } catch (_error) {
    return false;
  }
}

function mapTimestampLabel(date = new Date()) {
  const pad = (value) => String(value).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())} ${pad(date.getHours())}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}`;
}

function renderSavedMaps(selectedId = savedMapSelect.value) {
  const maps = savedMaps().sort((a, b) => String(b.updatedAt || "").localeCompare(String(a.updatedAt || "")));
  savedMapSelect.innerHTML = "";
  if (maps.length === 0) {
    const option = document.createElement("option");
    option.value = "";
    option.textContent = "暂无保存地图";
    savedMapSelect.appendChild(option);
    return;
  }

  for (const item of maps) {
    const option = document.createElement("option");
    option.value = item.id;
    option.textContent = item.name;
    savedMapSelect.appendChild(option);
  }
  if (selectedId && maps.some((item) => item.id === selectedId)) {
    savedMapSelect.value = selectedId;
  }
}

function currentSaveName() {
  const typedName = mapNameInput.value.trim();
  if (typedName) return typedName;
  if (currentMapName && currentMapName !== "Demo") return currentMapName;
  return `未命名地图 ${mapTimestampLabel()}`;
}

function saveCurrentMap() {
  const name = currentSaveName();
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const data = { ...currentEditorMapData(), name };
  const maps = savedMaps();
  const existingIndex = maps.findIndex((item) => item.name === name);
  const existingId = existingIndex >= 0 ? maps[existingIndex].id : null;
  const record = {
    id: existingId || `map-${Date.now()}`,
    name,
    updatedAt: now,
    stampCount: stamps.length,
    data,
  };
  if (existingIndex >= 0) {
    maps[existingIndex] = record;
  } else {
    maps.push(record);
  }
  if (!writeSavedMaps(maps)) {
    statusText.textContent = "保存失败：当前浏览器不允许写入本地存储。请使用 http://127.0.0.1:8794/web/index.html 打开编辑器。";
    return;
  }
  currentMapName = name;
  mapNameInput.value = name;
  renderSavedMaps(record.id);
  statusText.textContent = `已保存地图「${name}」。每个不同名称会成为“我的地图”中的一个新地图。`;
}

function openSavedMap() {
  const id = savedMapSelect.value;
  const record = savedMaps().find((item) => item.id === id);

function deleteSavedMap() {
  const id = savedMapSelect.value;
  const record = savedMaps().find((item) => item.id === id);
  if (!record) {
    statusText.textContent = "没有可删除的保存地图。";
    return;
  }
  const maps = savedMaps().filter((item) => item.id !== id);
  if (!writeSavedMaps(maps)) {
    statusText.textContent = "删除失败：当前浏览器不允许写入本地存储。";
    return;
  }
  renderSavedMaps();
  statusText.textContent = `已删除保存地图「${record.name}」。`;
}

function stampTexturePath(stamp) {
  const asset = assetById(stamp.assetId);
  return asset?.file ? `res://${asset.file}` : "";
}

function godotMapData() {
  const groundId = defaultTile()?.id || GROUND_ASSET_ID;
  return {
    format: "GameGmetaGodotMap",
    version: 1,
    cols: COLS,
    rows: ROWS,
    cell: CELL,
    terrain: terrain.map((row) => row.map(() => {
      const asset = assetById(groundId);
      return {
        assetId: groundId,
        texture: asset?.file ? `res://${asset.file}` : "",
        blocking: false,
      };
    })),
    objects: stamps.map((stamp) => ({
      id: stamp.id,
      assetId: stamp.assetId || null,
      texture: stampTexturePath(stamp),
      x: stamp.x,
      y: stamp.y,
      w: stamp.w,
      h: stamp.h,
      blocking: Boolean(stamp.blocking),
      z: 6,
    })),
  };
}

function isBlockingAsset(asset) {
  if (!asset || asset.id === GROUND_ASSET_ID) return false;

    "tiny-town/Tiles/tile_0014",

[54] user: 样板森林边缘：按 Sample 左侧森林的单株树密排方式。
样板混合树组：按 Sample 中散树/小树林混排方式。
红顶蓝墙房屋：按 Sample 左上房屋组合。
灰顶木墙房屋：按 Sample 中部/右侧小屋组合。
城堡门楼：按 Sample 右下城堡的塔楼 + 中央门楼复合结构。
泥地区块：按 Sample 右上泥地块组合。和原图不同，需要每个板块和原图一致

[54] user: 样板森林边缘：按 Sample 左侧森林的单株树密排方式。
样板混合树组：按 Sample 中散树/小树林混排方式。
红顶蓝墙房屋：按 Sample 左上房屋组合。
灰顶木墙房屋：按 Sample 中部/右侧小屋组合。
城堡门楼：按 Sample 右下城堡的塔楼 + 中央门楼复合结构。
泥地区块：按 Sample 右上泥地块组合。和原图不同，需要每个板块和原图一致

[54] user: 样板森林边缘：按 Sample 左侧森林的单株树密排方式。
样板混合树组：按 Sample 中散树/小树林混排方式。
红顶蓝墙房屋：按 Sample 左上房屋组合。
灰顶木墙房屋：按 Sample 中部/右侧小屋组合。
城堡门楼：按 Sample 右下城堡的塔楼 + 中央门楼复合结构。
泥地区块：按 Sample 右上泥地块组合。和原图不同，需要每个板块和原图一致

[54] user: 样板森林边缘：按 Sample 左侧森林的单株树密排方式。
样板混合树组：按 Sample 中散树/小树林混排方式。
红顶蓝墙房屋：按 Sample 左上房屋组合。
灰顶木墙房屋：按 Sample 中部/右侧小屋组合。
城堡门楼：按 Sample 右下城堡的塔楼 + 中央门楼复合结构。
泥地区块：按 Sample 右上泥地块组合。和原图不同，需要每个板块和原图一致

[54] user: 样板森林边缘：按 Sample 左侧森林的单株树密排方式。
样板混合树组：按 Sample 中散树/小树林混排方式。
红顶蓝墙房屋：按 Sample 左上房屋组合。
灰顶木墙房屋：按 Sample 中部/右侧小屋组合。
城堡门楼：按 Sample 右下城堡的塔楼 + 中央门楼复合结构。
泥地区块：按 Sample 右上泥地块组合。和原图不同，需要每个板块和原图一致

[54] user: 样板森林边缘：按 Sample 左侧森林的单株树密排方式。
样板混合树组：按 Sample 中散树/小树林混排方式。
红顶蓝墙房屋：按 Sample 左上房屋组合。
灰顶木墙房屋：按 Sample 中部/右侧小屋组合。
城堡门楼：按 Sample 右下城堡的塔楼 + 中央门楼复合结构。
泥地区块：按 Sample 右上泥地块组合。和原图不同，需要每个板块和原图一致
